fills_per_year_90_day * patients_per_medication\n        annual_drug_cost_100_day = (price_per_1000_tablets_usd / 1000.0) * tablets_per_fill_100_day * fills_per_year_100_day * patients_per_medication\n\n        annual_supply_cost_90_day = vial_price_usd * patients_per_medication * fills_per_year_90_day\n        annual_supply_cost_100_day = vial_price_usd * patients_per_medication * fills_per_year_100_day\n\n        annual_reimbursement_90_day = reimbursement_per_fill_300_patients_usd * fills_per_year_90_day\n        annual_reimbursement_100_day = reimbursement_per_fill_300_patients_usd * fills_per_year_100_day\n\n        annual_revenue_90_day = annual_reimbursement_90_day - annual_drug_cost_90_day - annual_supply_cost_90_day\n        annual_revenue_100_day = annual_reimbursement_100_day - annual_drug_cost_100_day - annual_supply_cost_100_day\n        annual_revenue_difference = annual_revenue_100_day - annual_revenue_90_day\n\n
